# Graph Report - thingtime  (2026-10-17)

## Corpus Check
- 3645 files · ~9,777,167 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.

## Summary
- 50105 nodes · 131193 edges · 1493 communities (1231 shown, 262 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 98% EXTRACTED · 2% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 2876 edges (avg confidence: 0.68)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `527063c6`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
